aor1776_hre_initialize = {

 if = {
  limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_initialized } exists = c:AUS }
  set_global_variable = aor1776_hre_initialized
  set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_emperor_country value = c:AUS }
  c:AUS = { every_scope_character = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_emperor_character } set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_emperor_person value = this } } }
  every_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes } set_variable = aor1776_hre_guarantees_privileges }
  c:BAV ?= { set_variable = aor1776_hre_wittelsbach }
  c:KPF ?= { set_variable = aor1776_hre_wittelsbach }
c:AUS ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_catholic_court
}
c:BAV ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_catholic_court
}
c:KPF ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_catholic_court
}
c:SAX ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_catholic_court
}
c:KMZ ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_catholic_court
}
c:KTR ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_catholic_court
}
c:KCL ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_catholic_court
}
c:KMZ ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_spiritual_elector
}
c:KTR ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_spiritual_elector
}
c:KCL ?= {
set_variable = aor1776_hre_spiritual_elector
}

  aor1776_hre_refresh_pacts = yes
 }


aor1776_hre_feedback_refresh = yes

aor1776_hre_restore_membership = yes
aor1776_hre_schedule_membership_guard = yes

aor1776_hre_reform_controller = yes
}
aor1776_hre_refresh_pacts = {

 if = { limit = { exists = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country }
  every_country = {
   limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes NOT = { aor1776_hre_emperor = yes } NOT = { has_diplomatic_pact = { who = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country type = aor1776_imperial_obligation } } }
   create_diplomatic_pact = { country = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country type = aor1776_imperial_obligation }
  }
 }

}

aor1776_hre_release_imperial_subjects = {

 every_country = { remove_variable = aor1776_hre_release_selected }
 every_subject_or_below = { limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes } set_variable = aor1776_hre_release_selected }
 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_release_selected } make_independent = yes }
 every_country = {
  limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_release_selected }
  save_scope_as = hre_holder
  aor1776_hre_return_released_subject_provinces = yes
  if = { limit = { NOT = { is_in_same_power_bloc = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country } global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country = { is_power_bloc_leader = yes power_bloc ?= { has_identity = identity:identity_aor1776_imperial_federation } } }
   join_power_bloc = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country
  }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { is_in_same_power_bloc = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country } } set_variable = aor1776_hre_release_bloc_pending }
  else = { remove_variable = aor1776_hre_release_bloc_pending }
 }
 every_country = { remove_variable = aor1776_hre_release_selected }
 remove_variable = aor1776_hre_release_refused
 remove_variable = aor1776_hre_release_wait
 aor1776_hre_refresh_pacts = yes


aor1776_hre_feedback_refresh = yes
}

aor1776_hre_record_release_refusal = {

 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_release_penalty } }
  change_infamy = 5 save_scope_as = hre_reply_new_infamy_holder
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_release_penalty days = 3650 }
 }
 set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_release_refused days = 3650 }
 set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_release_wait days = 3650 }

}
aor1776_hre_open_election = {

 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open } }
  set_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open
  remove_global_variable = aor1776_hre_runoff
  aor1776_hre_start_ballot = yes
 }

}
aor1776_hre_start_ballot = {

 set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_elector_count value = 0 }
 set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ballots_cast value = 0 }
 every_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes } set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_votes value = 0 } remove_variable = aor1776_hre_voted remove_variable = aor1776_hre_voted_for remove_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round }
 global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_deliberation days = 30 } trigger_event = { id = aor1776_hre.3 days = 30 popup = no } }
 every_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_elector = yes } set_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_vote_deadline days = 30 } change_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_elector_count add = 1 } }
 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round aor1776_hre_elector = yes } aor1776_hre_register_initial_ballot = yes }
 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round is_ai = no } trigger_event = { id = aor1776_hre.1 popup = yes } }
 aor1776_hre_feedback_refresh = yes

}
aor1776_hre_ai_ballot = {

 save_scope_as = hre_voter
 if = { limit = { exists = var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate
  var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes } }
  var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate = { save_scope_as = hre_vote_choice }
  aor1776_hre_cast_vote = yes
 }
 else = {
  ordered_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes }
   order_by = aor1776_hre_vote_preference position = 0 check_range_bounds = no
   save_scope_as = hre_vote_choice
   scope:hre_voter = { aor1776_hre_cast_vote = yes }
  }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_cast_vote = {

 if = { limit = { aor1776_hre_elector = yes has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open
  has_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round
  exists = scope:hre_vote_choice scope:hre_vote_choice = { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes }
  OR = { NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_voted }
   global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country ?= { has_variable = aor1776_hre_deliberation }
  }
  OR = { NOT = { exists = var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate }
   var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= scope:hre_vote_choice
   var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = no }
   is_ai = no
  }
 }
  if = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate
   var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes }
   NOT = { var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= scope:hre_vote_choice }
  }
   change_infamy = 10
   remove_variable = aor1776_hre_promised_candidate
   remove_variable = aor1776_hre_promise_cast
  }
  remove_variable = aor1776_hre_promise_cast
  if = { limit = { var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= scope:hre_vote_choice } set_variable = aor1776_hre_promise_cast }
  set_variable = aor1776_hre_voted
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_voted_for value = scope:hre_vote_choice }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate value = scope:hre_vote_choice }
  # Rebuild totals from one stored ballot per elector; changing a vote never
  # increments the elector count or leaves the former candidate's vote behind.
  aor1776_hre_recount_ballot = yes
  aor1776_hre_update_vote_preview = yes
 }

}
aor1776_hre_recount_ballot = {

 set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_elector_count value = 0 }
 set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ballots_cast value = 0 }
 every_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes } set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_votes value = 0 } }
 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round aor1776_hre_elector = yes }
  change_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_elector_count add = 1 }
  if = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_voted exists = var:aor1776_hre_voted_for var:aor1776_hre_voted_for = { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes } }
   change_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ballots_cast add = 1 }
   var:aor1776_hre_voted_for = { change_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_votes add = 1 } }
  }
  else = { remove_variable = aor1776_hre_voted }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_tally_ballot = {

 if = { limit = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open } aor1776_hre_recount_ballot = yes }
 if = { limit = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open NOT = { global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_deliberation } } global_var:aor1776_hre_elector_count > 0 global_var:aor1776_hre_ballots_cast >= global_var:aor1776_hre_elector_count }
  ordered_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes } order_by = var:aor1776_hre_votes position = 0 check_range_bounds = no save_scope_as = hre_elected_country }
  if = { limit = { exists = scope:hre_elected_country }
   scope:hre_elected_country = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_twice_votes value = var:aor1776_hre_votes } change_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_twice_votes multiply = 2 } }
   if = { limit = { scope:hre_elected_country = { var:aor1776_hre_twice_votes > global_var:aor1776_hre_elector_count } }
    scope:hre_elected_country = { aor1776_hre_install_elected_country = yes }
   }
   else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_runoff } }
    every_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes } remove_variable = aor1776_hre_finalist }
    ordered_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_candidate = yes } order_by = var:aor1776_hre_votes position = 0 check_range_bounds = no set_variable = aor1776_hre_finalist }
    ordered_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_candidate = yes } order_by = var:aor1776_hre_votes position = 1 check_range_bounds = no set_variable = aor1776_hre_finalist }
    set_global_variable = aor1776_hre_runoff
    aor1776_hre_start_ballot = yes
   }
   else = { set_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_deadlocked } # Never install a mere plurality or break a tie silently.
  }
 }

}

remove_variable = aor1776_hre_saved_aor1776_hre_administration_2
remove_variable = aor1776_hre_saved_aor1776_hre_administration_1
  scope:hre_old_imperial_country.power_bloc ?= {
   aor1776_hre_track_principle_order = yes
   aor1776_hre_snapshot_principles = yes
   every_power_bloc_member = { set_variable = aor1776_hre_transfer_member }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_pending NOT = { this = scope:hre_old_imperial_country } }
   aor1776_hre_apply_succession_loss_to_snapshot = yes
  }
  set_variable = aor1776_hre_creation_permitted
  # No unverified leave/destroy effect and no destructive preamble. Native
  # create/join behavior for a founder already in a bloc requires engine testing.
  if = { limit = { is_direct_subject_of = scope:hre_old_imperial_country OR = { is_subject_type = subject_type_aor1776_hre_protectorate is_subject_type = subject_type_aor1776_hre_crownland } }
   if = { limit = { is_subject_type = subject_type_aor1776_hre_protectorate } set_variable = aor1776_hre_was_protectorate }
   else = { set_variable = aor1776_hre_was_crownland }
   make_independent = yes
  }
  create_power_bloc = {
   name = HOLY_ROMAN_PB map_color = { 219 211 157 } founding_date = 962.6.9
   identity = identity_aor1776_imperial_federation
  }
  if = { limit = { is_power_bloc_leader = yes power_bloc ?= { has_identity = identity:identity_aor1776_imperial_federation } }
   power_bloc = { aor1776_hre_restore_principles = yes }
   if = { limit = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_reform_stage global_var:aor1776_hre_reform_stage > 0 }
    set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_emperor_country value = this }
    if = { limit = { global_var:aor1776_hre_reform_stage = 1 } aor1776_hre_bind_protectorate = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { global_var:aor1776_hre_reform_stage = 2 } aor1776_hre_bind_crownland = yes }
   }
   remove_variable = aor1776_hre_was_protectorate remove_variable = aor1776_hre_was_crownland
   every_country = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_transfer_member NOT = { this = scope:hre_elected_country } } join_power_bloc = scope:hre_elected_country }
   every_country = { limit = { has_diplomatic_pact = { who = scope:hre_old_imperial_country type = aor1776_imperial_obligation } } remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = scope:hre_old_imperial_country type = aor1776_imperial_obligation } }
   aor1776_hre_finish_investiture = yes
  }
  else = { set_global_variable = aor1776_hre_native_transfer_blocked
   if = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_was_protectorate }
    scope:hre_old_imperial_country = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = scope:hre_elected_country type = aor1776_hre_protectorate } }
   }
   else_if = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_was_crownland }
    scope:hre_old_imperial_country = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = scope:hre_elected_country type = aor1776_hre_crownland } }
   }
   remove_variable = aor1776_hre_was_protectorate remove_variable = aor1776_hre_was_crownland
  } # Keep old bloc and election ledger; do not fake success.
  remove_variable = aor1776_hre_creation_permitted
  every_country = { remove_variable = aor1776_hre_transfer_member }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_finish_investiture = {

 if = { limit = { ruler ?= { is_character_alive = yes is_female = no age >= 18 NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_deceased_emperor } } }
  ruler = { set_variable = aor1776_hre_emperor_character set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_emperor_person value = this } }
 }
 else = { heir ?= { set_variable = aor1776_hre_emperor_character set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_emperor_person value = this } } }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { this = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country } }
  every_country = { limit = { has_diplomatic_pact = { who = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country type = aor1776_imperial_obligation } } remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country type = aor1776_imperial_obligation } }
 }
 set_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_emperor_country value = this }
 remove_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open
 remove_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_deadlocked
 remove_global_variable = aor1776_hre_native_transfer_blocked
 remove_global_variable = aor1776_hre_runoff
 every_country = { remove_variable = aor1776_hre_finalist remove_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round remove_variable = aor1776_hre_voted remove_variable = aor1776_hre_voted_for remove_variable = aor1776_hre_vote_deadline }
 remove_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_pending
 aor1776_hre_refresh_pacts = yes


every_country = { limit = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_promise_cast } remove_variable = aor1776_hre_promised_candidate remove_variable = aor1776_hre_promise_cast }

aor1776_hre_feedback_refresh = yes

aor1776_hre_restore_membership = yes
aor1776_hre_schedule_membership_guard = yes

aor1776_hre_reform_controller = yes
}

 every_country = {
  every_scope_state = {
   if = { limit = { var:aor1776_hre_registered_count > 0 }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_modifier = aor1776_hre_registered_territory } } add_modifier = { name = aor1776_hre_registered_territory } }
    scope:hre_current_emperor = { add_to_variable_list = { name = aor1776_hre_states_list target = prev } }
   }
   else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = aor1776_hre_registered_territory } remove_modifier = aor1776_hre_registered_territory }
  }
  if = { limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes }
   set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_relation_to_emperor value = "relations(scope:hre_current_emperor)" }
   if = { limit = { aor1776_hre_elector = yes }
    scope:hre_current_emperor = { add_to_variable_list = { name = aor1776_hre_electors_list target = prev } }
 aor1776_hre_update_vote_preview = yes
    if = { limit = { var:aor1776_hre_relation_to_emperor < 0 } change_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_hostile_electors add = 1 } }
   }
   if = { limit = { aor1776_hre_free_city = yes } scope:hre_current_emperor = { add_to_variable_list = { name = aor1776_hre_free_cities_list target = prev } } }
 if = { limit = { aor1776_hre_free_city = yes var:aor1776_hre_relation_to_emperor < 0 } change_global_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_hostile_cities add = 1 } }
  }
 }
 every_country = { limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ui_emperor value = scope:hre_current_emperor }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ui_person value = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_person }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_outside_provinces value = global_var:aor1776_hre_outside_provinces }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_under_authority value = 427 }
  change_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_under_authority subtract = global_var:aor1776_hre_outside_provinces }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_hostile_electors value = global_var:aor1776_hre_hostile_electors }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_hostile_cities value = global_var:aor1776_hre_hostile_cities }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_aor1776_hre_situation } } add_journal_entry = { type = je_aor1776_hre_situation } }
  if = { limit = { aor1776_hre_elector = yes has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_aor1776_hre_vote } } add_journal_entry = { type = je_aor1776_hre_vote } }
 }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_restore_membership = {

 if = { limit = { exists = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country
  global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country = { is_power_bloc_leader = yes power_bloc ?= { has_identity = identity:identity_aor1776_imperial_federation } } }
  global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country = { save_scope_as = hre_guard_emperor }
  every_country = {
   limit = { aor1776_hre_member = yes is_subject = no
    NOT = { this = scope:hre_guard_emperor }
    NOT = { is_in_same_power_bloc = scope:hre_guard_emperor }
    NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = scope:hre_guard_emperor }
   }
   set_variable = aor1776_hre_membership_pending
   join_power_bloc = scope:hre_guard_emperor
   if = { limit = { is_in_same_power_bloc = scope:hre_guard_emperor }
    remove_variable = aor1776_hre_membership_pending
    if = { limit = { is_ai = no NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_membership_notice } }
     set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_membership_notice days = 90 }
     trigger_event = { id = aor1776_hre.41 popup = yes }
    }
   }
  }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_schedule_membership_guard = {

 if = { limit = { exists = global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country }
  global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country = {
   if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_membership_guard_scheduled } }
    set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_membership_guard_scheduled days = 2 }
    trigger_event = { id = aor1776_hre.40 days = 1 popup = no }
   }
  }
 }

}

aor1776_hre_prepare_liberation_goals = {
if = { limit = { is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_aor1776_hre_subject_release initiator = { aor1776_hre_emperor = yes }
 NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_liberation_prepared } }
 initiator = { save_scope_as = initiator } target = { save_scope_as = target save_scope_as = hre_foreign_holder }
 if = { limit = { scope:target = { aor1776_hre_member = yes is_subject = yes } } scope:target.top_overlord = { save_scope_as = hre_foreign_holder } }
 set_variable = aor1776_hre_liberation_prepared
if = { limit = { c:AUS ?= { aor1776_hre_member = yes is_subject_of = scope:hre_foreign_holder } }
   c:AUS = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_liberation_holder value = scope:hre_foreign_holder days = 3650 } }
   # Repair old mistaken backing once, not on each join notification.
   if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_AUS } }
    set_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_AUS
    if = { limit = { c:AUS = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:initiator } } remove_initiator_backers = { c:AUS } }
    if = { limit = { c:AUS = { NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:target } } } add_target_backers = { c:AUS } }
   }
  }
if = { limit = { c:KPF ?= { aor1776_hre_member = yes is_subject_of = scope:hre_foreign_holder } }
   c:KPF = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_liberation_holder value = scope:hre_foreign_holder days = 3650 } }
   # Repair old mistaken backing once, not on each join notification.
   if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_KPF } }
    set_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_KPF
    if = { limit = { c:KPF = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:initiator } } remove_initiator_backers = { c:KPF } }
    if = { limit = { c:KPF = { NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:target } } } add_target_backers = { c:KPF } }
   }
  }
if = { limit = { c:KTR ?= { aor1776_hre_member = yes is_subject_of = scope:hre_foreign_holder } }
   c:KTR = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_liberation_holder value = scope:hre_foreign_holder days = 3650 } }
   # Repair old mistaken backing once, not on each join notification.
   if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_KTR } }
    set_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_KTR
    if = { limit = { c:KTR = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:initiator } } remove_initiator_backers = { c:KTR } }
    if = { limit = { c:KTR = { NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:target } } } add_target_backers = { c:KTR } }
   }
  }
if = { limit = { c:KMZ ?= { aor1776_hre_member = yes is_subject_of = scope:hre_foreign_holder } }
   c:KMZ = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_liberation_holder value = scope:hre_foreign_holder days = 3650 } }
   # Repair old mistaken backing once, not on each join notification.
   if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_KMZ } }
    set_variable = aor1776_hre_captive_corrected_KMZ
    if = { limit = { c:KMZ = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:initiator } } remove_initiator_backers = { c:KMZ } }
    if = { limit = { c:KMZ = { NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = scope:target } } } add_target_backers = { c:KMZ } }
   }
  }
if = { limit = { c:KCL ?= { aor1776_hre_member = yes is_subject_of = scope:hre_foreign_holder } }
   c:KCL = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_liberation_holder value = scope:hre_foreign_holder days = 3650 } }
   # Repair old mistaken backing once, not on each join notification.

aor1776_hre_update_vote_preview = {
save_scope_as = hre_voter
 remove_variable = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_choice
 remove_variable = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_cast
 remove_variable = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_promised
 remove_variable = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_selected
 if = { limit = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open has_variable = aor1776_hre_voted exists = var:aor1776_hre_voted_for }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_choice value = var:aor1776_hre_voted_for }
  set_variable = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_cast
 }
 else_if = { limit = { exists = var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes } }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_choice value = var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate }
  set_variable = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_promised
 }
 else_if = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = var:aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate
  var:aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_candidate = yes } }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_choice value = var:aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate }
  set_variable = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_selected
 }
 else_if = { limit = { is_ai = yes }
  ordered_country = {
   limit = { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes }
   order_by = aor1776_hre_vote_preference position = 0 check_range_bounds = no
   scope:hre_voter = { set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_ui_vote_choice value = prev } }
  }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_choose_electoral_vote = {

 if = { limit = { aor1776_hre_can_choose_electoral_vote = { CANDIDATE = scope:hre_vote_choice } }
  set_variable = { name = aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate value = scope:hre_vote_choice }
  if = { limit = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open }
   aor1776_hre_cast_vote = yes
   aor1776_hre_tally_ballot = yes
  }
  else = { aor1776_hre_update_vote_preview = yes }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_update_unpledged_ballots = {

 if = { limit = { has_global_variable = aor1776_hre_election_open
  global_var:aor1776_hre_emperor_country ?= { has_variable = aor1776_hre_deliberation } }
  every_country = { limit = { is_ai = yes aor1776_hre_elector = yes
   has_variable = aor1776_hre_voter_this_round
   NOT = { var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes } }
  } aor1776_hre_ai_ballot = yes }
 }

}
aor1776_hre_register_initial_ballot = {

 if = { limit = { exists = var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate
  var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes } }
  var:aor1776_hre_promised_candidate = { save_scope_as = hre_vote_choice }
  aor1776_hre_cast_vote = yes
 }
 else_if = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = var:aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate
  var:aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate ?= { aor1776_hre_ballot_candidate = yes } }
  var:aor1776_hre_preferred_candidate = { save_scope_as = hre_vote_choice }
  aor1776_hre_cast_vote = yes
 }
 else_if = { limit = { is_ai = yes } aor1776_hre_ai_ballot = yes }

}
